- [Changed] Ship a smaller plugin ZIP. `dist/index.js.map` is 1.8x the bundle it describes and embeds the whole TypeScript source, and the `xml/dom` and `xml/sax` CPython fallback packages are reachable from nothing in the plugin or its vendored dependencies; neither is installed any more. The map stays in the repository for anyone debugging a released build. The package is 12% smaller.
- [Changed] Delete the Steam launch-option integration. CE Decky starts Cheat Engine as a process it owns inside the running game's Proton prefix, so nothing ever called the launch-option writer: `writeLaunchOptionsVerified` had one caller chain and it ended at a campaign harness that was never wired into the plugin. Gone with it are the launch-option parser/editor, the ownership and recovery journal, the `shortcuts.vdf` writer, `prepare_launch_bundle`, both launch-ownership RPCs and the profile field behind them.
- [Security] Make "CE Decky never writes Steam state" checkable rather than a promise: `scripts/verify_repo.py` now fails the build if `SetAppLaunchOptions` or `SetShortcutLaunchOptions` appears anywhere under `src/`.
- [Fixed] Load a profile written by an earlier version unchanged, and stop writing its `launch_ownership` key. A profile whose record is non-null is rejected with an explicit reason instead of being silently emptied, because this build can no longer restore the Steam state it describes.
- [Changed] Remove six superseded development files ahead of the public release. `scripts/bootstrap.sh`, `scripts/build.sh` and `scripts/test.sh` were unreferenced one-line wrappers around `scripts/qa.py`, which is the only ordinary validation entrypoint; `scripts/test_ts_core.sh` needed a global `tsc` and was replaced by `scripts/test_ts_core.mjs` on the pinned toolchain; `scripts/build_prebuilt_fallback.cjs` was the offline bundle builder that `scripts/frontend_fallback.py` replaced and nothing ran; `src/types.d.ts` declared `SteamClient` globals no production code reads. `scripts/audit.sh` joins the temporary campaign material because the prototype archive is its only remaining purpose.
- [Changed] Rewrite `README.md` for the people who will read it in a public repository: what the plugin does, what it needs, how to install it, and how the whole loop works from the controller, with the limitations stated plainly instead of implied. It no longer describes the pre-launch behaviour the controller workflow replaced, and no longer references the temporary campaign material.
- [Changed] Re-read a cached FearLess listing page once a day instead of never. Freshness is tracked per page, so the daily pass fetches only what actually aged out and refreshes the index in place; the newest pages, which every table search already re-reads, are never fetched twice.
- [New] Report the FearLess index in Advanced → Debug details: how many pages and topics are held, when the whole index was last fully refreshed, how many pages are due, and how many pages the last background pass actually fetched.
- [Changed] Renumber the release history so every version reads in order. The 0.80.x/0.90.0 line becomes 0.8.x/0.9.0 and the releases before it are renumbered to stay below it; no entry was removed. Five existing commit messages still name the old numbering, and the publication instruction in `AGENTS.md` now starts the public repository from a fresh initial commit, which removes that and the campaign material from history rather than only from the tip.

## 0.8.1 — 2026-08-29

- [Changed] Drop the Online Sandbox publication workflow and its `tools/sandbox_publish.py` machinery. Development uses one ordinary Git worktree, and an unusable checkout or transport is reported rather than routed around.
- [Changed] Declare the campaign runbook, status ledger, gate contract, research directories and target harness as temporary material in `AGENTS.md`, with the instruction to delete them - and every reference to them - before the repository is made public.
- [Changed] Replace every real game, folder, table and author name in tests, fixtures and comments with synthetic equivalents that keep the exact property each case proves. Release-group suffixes stay and are now covered as a structural shape rather than an enumerated list.
- [Changed] Drop the anti-bot challenge-bypass prohibition from the project documents. Provider behaviour is unchanged: a challenged route still becomes the exact source-page browser handoff.
- [Fixed] Hold a cancelled unload until every native worker it cannot stop has finished. A single shield only survives the first cancellation, so a second one abandoned exactly the extraction, promotion, cleanup, unlink and index-cache workers it existed to drain.
- [Fixed] Close the service even when the operation drain is cancelled or fails. Retiring owned Cheat Engine processes, staged provider files and network sessions is what unload is for, and one cancelled drain skipped all of it.
- [Security] Re-check a promoted Cheat Engine archive import against its manifest's own file and byte totals, and bound the file/directory tree an archive expands to while it is being inspected.
- [Fixed] Make panel runtime controls safe for nested tables: a pinned child enables its enclosing scripts first, and Disable all retires children before the scripts that created them.
- [Fixed] Make stopped-session configuration reflect the effective startup state and enable auto-load for value-only or explicit-off choices, not only active cheats.
- [Security] Snapshot one stable bounded Cheat Engine archive before inspection, hashing and extraction; validate its provenance manifest and restore a prior installation before fallible cleanup.
- [Fixed] Drain native launch/acquisition mutations through cancellation, always release attached-launch reservations, and prevent browser-handoff acquisitions from appearing after unload.
- [Security] Fail closed when the live Wine prefix, launch-record directory or baseline game PID state is unobservable; an unreadable process is no longer mistaken for a game exit.
- [Security] Bound PE reads and every file/directory inventory used by runtime, managed-install and removal validation; reject bidirectional controls in imported version labels.
- [Fixed] Preserve React's fragment/key hierarchy for controller action groups so duplicate synthesized keys cannot reuse or omit an action.

## 0.8.0 — 2026-08-29

The version jumps from 0.5.1 because what changed is the shape of the project, not another increment: the controller workflow is complete end to end, and the thirteen enumerated target gates are down to the two that a developer machine genuinely cannot answer. What stands between here and 1.0 is proving those two on the target, not a backlog.

- [Changed] Reduce the target gate set from thirteen gates to two. C03 (live cheat control in a running game) absorbs T7 and T8, which exercise the same loop; T9 (getting a table on this OS) absorbs T2, because an archive only ever arrives as a provider download or a user import; T0, T1, C01 and T10 are met by accumulated evidence and repeated packaged installs.
- [Changed] Retire T3, T4, T5 and T6. They validate CE Decky writing Steam launch options so Steam carries Cheat Engine into a game it is starting, and the product does not work that way: it attaches to a game already running as its own owned process group, and `src/steam/launchPlan.ts` has exactly one consumer - the campaign harness, which is not wired into the plugin. The ownership and recovery contract stays implemented and tested, unexercised. Retiring them also removes the only paths that could leave Steam state owned, so no gate the product still has to pass can end with an unrestored mutation.
- [New] Configure cheats with Cheat Engine stopped. A game can be set up before it is started: the picker shows what this exact table is configured to do, Apply saves the selection rather than writing to a session, and it switches auto-load on by itself, because a selection made with nothing running only ever reaches Cheat Engine that way.
- [Changed] Switching a cheat on switches on the scripts that create it, in outermost-first order, instead of refusing the change and naming a parent to find. A Cheat Engine record inside a script has no address until that script has run, both at Apply and when auto-load restores a remembered selection.
- [Fixed] Keep a value the user pre-set before the enclosing script is on. It cannot be written yet, which is a pre-game setting rather than an error: the typed choice is stored for that exact table and written once the script is enabled.
- [Changed] Hide the scripts a table uses to build its cheats behind a `Scripts` toggle in the picker header. They are machinery CE Decky switches on by itself, and listing them beside the cheats doubled the list to page through.
- [New] Import a Cheat Engine installation packed into a `.zip`, for when the official installer is no longer downloadable. The archive is validated before a byte is written, the installation root is located inside whatever nesting the packing produced, the tree is checked for the files a Cheat Engine installation must have, and promotion is atomic into a plugin-owned directory with its own manifest.
- [New] Read the registered Cheat Engine's version from its own PE version resource and show it on the panel. The managed release knows its version from the pinned manifest; anything imported declares one in its resources or declares none, and not knowing is a missing label rather than an import failure.
- [New] Add `Disable all`, which switches every active cheat off without stopping Cheat Engine, and remembers that choice.
- [New] Report the observed Proton identity, the resolved compatdata directory, the game's own Wine prefix and its running Windows executables in Advanced. An attached launch depends entirely on these three facts and none of them were visible when one was missing.
- [New] Report the active table's provenance in Advanced - provider, source page, date, stored path and authorization state - with the source page openable in the Steam browser.
- [New] Report what CE Decky has put on disk, directory by directory, with file counts, sizes and what each directory is for. Removal readiness promised this and only ever answered whether deletion was allowed.
- [Fixed] Stop removal readiness reporting a prepared session as a blocker. A prepared session is plugin-owned state that removal deletes anyway; what makes deletion unsafe is a Cheat Engine process CE Decky owns still running, which is now what is checked.
- [Fixed] Make the file picker usable. It was opened with a page size of one, so it listed a single entry per directory however many it held, and with folders excluded, so there was nowhere to navigate. Decky's last argument is a page size, not a selection limit.
- [Fixed] Repeat an Apply failure beside the Apply button. Apply sits at the bottom of a scrolling list, so the failure reported at the top is off screen by the time it is pressed.
- [Fixed] Open an external page after dismissing the dialog. Steam navigates underneath a Decky modal, so the browser opened with its chrome visible around a dialog that was still covering its page.
- [Changed] Draw CE Decky's own section headings, with the rule beside the heading rather than under it, and pull row separators in from both edges so the only line reaching them divides two sections. Steam's section classes are content hashes on the shipped client, so they cannot be styled by name.
- [Fixed] Stop a long label widening a row and pushing its own controls past the panel edge; the label column may now shrink, so it is cut short by the ellipsis it already asks for.
- [Fixed] Preserve filename case when extracting an imported Cheat Engine archive. Matching is case-insensitive; writing was too, which renamed `Cheat Engine.exe` and every mixed-case asset beside it.
- [Changed] Rename **Choose cheats…** to **Configure cheats**, and tidy Advanced: the game selector is gone because the panel already picks the game, the legacy startup row appears only when there is something to clear, and Revoke sits on the authorization row it acts on.
- [Limitation] Writing MemoryRecords in a running game, stopping by exact descriptor and switching table without ending the game remain unproven on the target; reading all 17 supported records of the reviewed table is proven. Providers on SteamOS also await a retest after the Playground size-metadata correction.

## 0.5.1 — 2026-08-23

- [Fixed] Make a lone button on a row actually respond. Steam's `Focusable` is itself a focus target, so wrapping a single control in one let it take the focus while its activation did nothing: **Choose game** looked focused and silently ignored every press. Only two or more controls are grouped now.
- [Fixed] Keep an explicit game choice after the panel closes. Opening any Decky modal unmounts the quick-access panel, so a game picked while nothing was running vanished the moment the picker closed. The exact AppID is remembered and restored, but only while no game is running and only if that library entry still exists; a running game supersedes it and clears it for good.
- [Fixed] Stop Cheat Engine's `??` placeholder from eating a typed value. A record whose script is not enabled yet reads back as unreadable, and adopting that answer replaced the value on screen and persisted it as the remembered choice for the next session.
- [New] Refuse a value write that cannot land, naming the control to switch on first: a value inside a Cheat Engine script has no address until that script is enabled. The expanded row says so before Apply is reached, and a failed verification now names the cheat and what Cheat Engine answered instead of a bare MemoryRecord ID.
- [New] Offer to apply staged cheats when leaving the picker. The header counts unapplied changes and Back opens an Apply / Discard / Keep editing choice, so work is not lost when the Apply button has scrolled out of view.
- [New] Show a pinned control's value on the panel, falling back to the table's confirmed choice while Cheat Engine still reports the address as unreadable.
- [New] Add **Debug details** to Advanced: the backend's diagnostics snapshot - uptime, storage counts, per-provider state and counters, session inventory, persisted-state errors and capability flags - was produced all along and never displayed.
- [Changed] Compact Advanced / Diagnostics: two fewer sections, every action sized to its text and placed on the row of the state it acts on, and a `?` on each row that explains in plain language what that row is for and when it is needed.
- [Fixed] Keep every quick-access row inside the panel. Steam's `compact` field padding is a full-bleed variant in that column: it removes the inline padding and pulls the row 16px outside the section, so labels hung off the left edge and a row's own buttons were clipped by the right one. Rows now use the standard horizontal padding and take their vertical tightening from CE Decky's own scoped rules, and a secondary button is sized by its text instead of growing to fill its line.
- [Changed] Rebuild the quick-access panel around the cheats. Cheat Engine, the current game and the selected table are now one row each carrying their own small action instead of nine rows and five section headings, rare actions no longer own a full-width button, long names cost one line, and Steam's own field metrics are tightened through its real runtime class names scoped to this plugin. The cheats the panel exists to control are reachable without scrolling.
- [Fixed] Stop Steam's gamepad toggle rendering clipped and overlapping the control beside it. It is a fixed 38x22 box with an absolutely positioned knob, so a flex row's default shrink narrowed the box while the knob kept its geometry.
- [Changed] Reveal a cheat's value editor as soon as that cheat is switched on, because a value or dropdown record does nothing until a value is written; script records, which are complete once active, stay one row. Cheat Engine's literal `??` for a record it cannot read yet no longer appears as a value.
- [Fixed] Start Cheat Engine again after it has already run once. Cheat Engine writes into its own installation directory (the bundled `ceshare` extension creates `autorun/ceshare/processlist.txt` at startup), which permanently invalidated the exact fingerprint of the promoted private runtime snapshot, so every launch after the first failed closed with no way for a normal user to recover. That tree is plugin-owned and fully reconstructible, so it is now rebuilt from the freshly SHA-verified staging copy, and only while no Cheat Engine process CE Decky owns can be running.
- [Fixed] Show the reason in a notification instead of an empty one. Decky Loader 3.2.6 builds its Python error from a field its own backend does not send, so every backend exception reached the frontend with an empty message; CE Decky now recovers the exact text from the traceback the loader does forward and falls back to the exception class rather than showing nothing.
- [New] Load the already authorized table and start Cheat Engine directly from the panel. A selected table, its consent and its target process are durable, so a second session no longer has to search for and re-review the same table just to reach the runtime; when the action is unavailable the panel names the exact blocker.
- [Changed] Redesign the cheat-selection menu for the 1280x800 Game Mode viewport. Each cheat is one visually separated row carrying its name, its group breadcrumb and its Active toggle, with value editing, pinning and the raw record ID behind that row's **More**; a page is six records and the pager is one compact row, so the modal no longer overflows the screen and clips its own title.
- [New] Pinning a control now promotes it onto the CE Decky panel with a live toggle, applied and remembered exactly as the picker's Apply does, instead of only creating a filter section inside the picker.
- [Changed] Report bounded AddressList count and exact table-path match diagnostics so an attached launch can distinguish an unloaded command-line table from exact MemoryRecord lookup failure.
- [Fixed] Keep Review's process selector on the exact running executable when a table hint differs from the observed Windows basename only in letter case.
- [Fixed] Repair a saved launcher target in Review when fresh exact observation identifies that executable as the game's launcher and another game process is running beside it.
- [Fixed] Stop presenting attachment sizes such as 78.21 KiB and 505.53 KiB as table versions, keep provider cancellation as cancellation instead of a blank failure, and bind reused directory-backed searches to the shortcut target they were produced from.
- [Changed] Require the installed-library provider survey after every ranking, alias or search-identity change, with per-game count comparison rather than only an aggregate result.
- [New] Retry a search that found nothing downloadable against the directory the game was installed into, when the library entry abbreviates or misspells its name. The library name stays the only identity while it finds something, so a folder naming a different game, such as a mod inside its base game's directory, can never outrank the entry the user chose. Across the games installed on the reviewed machine this turns `SRO: Hidden Blade` from no results into 21, `NX: Crimson Tide Resynced` into 16, and finds Neon Bazaar Kyoto for a shortcut named `Neon Bazar Kyoto`; build output, library roots, packaging words and emulator launcher paths are not names.
- [Fixed] Show a result's version next to its date instead of only when there is no date. One forum post commonly carries every revision of the same table, so six results shared a filename and a date and the version was the only thing telling them apart.
- [New] Add `scripts/provider_search_survey.py`, which runs the production table search for the games installed on this machine so search quality can be measured across a real library rather than sampled one game at a time.
- [Fixed] Stop a save file or a trainer from reaching a table search. Playground's `table` category is not table-only: a game with no Cheat Engine table still has its trainers and saves listed there, so each entry is now read through the category it declares, and a listing that carried entries and no table reports no tables instead of falling back to the unfiltered ranking.
- [Changed] Search providers by the name the library shows for the game rather than by its executable, which is named for whatever the build happens to be. A repack of `Cadence: A Silent Requiem` ships `Cadence.exe`, and the alias `cadence` scored the unrelated `Cadence of Fortune` at 0.808 against a 0.55 floor, filling a search for a 2026 game with results for a 2010 one. An entry with no usable name at all still falls back to its executable.
- [Fixed] Join the X display the running game itself reports when attaching. Gamescope runs two XWayland servers and Steam and the game do not share one, so Cheat Engine started on the session's display operated on window identities from another server and ended on the Xlib default error handler before its bridge could connect.
- [New] Observe the executable Steam asked Proton to run for the selected game from its own process table, so the target-process default can deprioritise a launcher for a Steam library entry and not only for a non-Steam shortcut, where Steam records the target in AppDetails.
- [New] Always preselect a target process in Review, ranked by how exact the evidence is: a process already confirmed for this game, a name the table carries that is also running, whatever is running that is not the game's own launch executable, and finally the table's hint when nothing is running. A hint no running process answers to no longer wins, because a table published for one store's build names that build's executable.
- [Fixed] Order process hints deterministically: two hints differing only in case previously left the order to set iteration, so the same table did not always inspect to the same result.
- [Fixed] Read process hints from Auto Assembler scripts as well as `Address` values, including Cheat Engine's own `{ Game : <exe> }` header and the `aobscanmodule` idiom where the module sits between two commas; eight of nine real tables sampled for the reviewed target name their process only there.
- [New] Offer the Windows `.exe` basenames the selected game is actually running when confirming a table's target process. The exact FearLess table for the reviewed game names no process at all, and the library entry points at a launcher rather than the game binary, so the only remaining option was typing a basename blind on a controller.
- [Changed] Reach the complete library picker as **Change game…** while a game is already selected, instead of only when detection found nothing; correcting a wrong automatic match no longer requires the Advanced screen.
- [Fixed] Clear retired damaged-table marks on an explicit fresh search, because a provider can replace a damaged upload with a fixed file under the same identity.
- [Changed] Drop the Wine and Proton runtime processes from the observed target-process choices: a live prefix reports Wine's services, Proton's helpers and the game's crash reporter beside the two executables that are actually the game. Anything running from the prefix's own Windows directory is dropped structurally, so a newer Proton cannot reintroduce noise under a name this build has never heard of, and the remainder is classified by the same exact-basename list already used for third-party store launchers.
- [New] Capture the composited Game Mode screen, including the Decky overlay, with `scripts/target_screenshot.py`; results are downscaled JPEGs under `/home/deck/Pictures/Screenshots/` with optional Quick-Access-panel and centered-modal crops.
- [Fixed] Report a downloaded table whose Cheat Engine XML is malformed as a damaged artifact instead of a last-step parser error: an exact Playground table closes a `CheatEntry` it never opened, so it matches its advertised SHA-256 and is still unusable. Structure is now checked while the file is being inspected, the acquisition retires with a message that names the file as damaged, and the catalog stops offering those exact bytes so a retry cannot cost another provider countdown for the same outcome.
- [Fixed] Drive the running-game repaint check with bounded polls instead of an exact poll count, so the workflow regression suite no longer fails intermittently on how many hydration round trips the panel happens to need.
- [Fixed] Keep a damaged table out of provider diagnostics: the source delivered exactly the bytes it advertised, so its status and cooldown no longer record an artifact defect as provider unavailability.
- [Changed] Add bounded structured lifecycle and failure logging across backend RPCs, provider work, imports, managed setup and CE launch, with correlation IDs, durations and safe unexpected-error trace frames while suppressing request-per-line HTTP noise and secrets.
- [Fixed] Treat Playground's advertised file size as display metadata rather than an integrity assertion; an exact target artifact delivered one byte fewer than the page claimed while still matching its advertised SHA-256 and passing table inspection.
- [Fixed] Preserve the primary Cheat Engine launch failure when stop or ownership-record cleanup also fails, while retaining the secondary cleanup detail in the diagnostic.
- [Fixed] Serialize provider import and cancellation, prompt for the common single encrypted archive member, and close Playground acquisition before the Search-to-Review focus handoff.
- [Fixed] Treat managed-CE cancellation after atomic promotion as a verified completion, remove finished UUID staging trees, and drain remaining subsystem owners even when one unload cleanup fails.
- [Security] Classify store launchers only by an exact known executable basename, never by a parent path that can contain a real game, and bound FearLess numeric topic identities in live and cached index input.
- [Changed] Show Playground's provider countdown and download in a separate cancellable controller modal, keeping rare archive member/password selection there while auto-importing the usual single table.
- [Fixed] Checkpoint and pause the resumable FearLess index on HTTP 429, then continue the missing listing page after the bounded provider cooldown instead of retrying aggressively.
- [Fixed] Accept Playground downloads from the exact additionally observed `dl3.gamedl.ru` CDN host while retaining an explicit no-wildcard artifact allowlist.
- [New] Discover FearLess tables through a resumable bounded background index of its readable table-forum listings, with visible page progress and no use of the challenged search route.
- [Fixed] Import a downloaded table immediately when it needs no archive choice and no password, instead of leaving a confirmation row below a long result list that the workflow silently waited on.
- [Fixed] Prefilter Playground's sitemap by an exact game token before scoring it: ranking all 50,000 entries cost over twenty seconds of every search for fifteen matches, and the gate changes no outcome.
- [Changed] Show each table's publication date and order results by recency inside a band of equally plausible matches, because match scores for one game cluster too tightly to order by.
- [Changed] Compact the source status to short names with counts, for example `FearLess: n/a · Playground: 3 · OpenCT: 0`.
- [New] Select the running game automatically even when a third-party store launcher is running beside it, by dropping known launcher executables from an ambiguous observation; a launcher-only observation still asks for a manual choice.
- [Changed] Offer only tables CE Decky can download itself and drop the browser handoff a controller cannot complete; the search reports every source it actually queried by name with its own count, so a provider that answers with nothing, one that is blocked, and a game with no tables are three visibly different outcomes.
- [Changed] Reuse the last table search for the same game and query instead of researching on every open, show the search age with an explicit **Search again**, and report elapsed seconds instead of an unbounded spinner.
- [Changed] Compact each catalog result to its title over one subdued metadata line, dropping the match percentage from the row.
- [Fixed] Search Playground through the game's own `table` category instead of its whole cheat sitemap, so trainers, save files, save editors and cheat mods no longer reach the results and only real tables cost a page fetch.
- [Fixed] Wire the temporary target-validation harness into **Advanced…** instead of above the home panel, where it pushed the Cheat Engine, game and table controls out of reach as soon as a game was selected.
- [New] Detect the running game on Steam builds that expose no running-app query to the frontend, using bounded backend candidates that are matched against the Steam library; prefix, Proton identity and launch path stay independently resolved.
- [Fixed] Read every live process record through the procfs-aware reader: the regular-file reader's bytes-read/size equality check rejects `/proc/<pid>/environ` and `/proc/<pid>/stat`, which silently reported that no game was running and could never prove owned Cheat Engine process identity on the real target.
- [Fixed] Reconcile Decky's expected socket closure and asynchronous tree replacement during plugin overwrite through bounded exact installed identity, then prove frontend restart by observed webhelper process replacement.
- [Fixed] Keep the home workflow usable after a managed reinstall: a superseded setup monitor can no longer repaint terminal progress over the cleared snapshot, a consumed operation is reported as unknown instead of a sticky completed state, a retained completed snapshot no longer hides **Reinstall CE** or disables game/table selection, and a capability refresh no longer overwrites the live monitor's own progress.
- [Fixed] Make exact managed-CE completion idempotent across overlapping Decky observers so a successful reinstall produces one non-empty notification without a second RPC error.
- [Changed] Add an exact-SHA Steam Machine Decky install harness that verifies the installed plugin tree and backend identity, then reloads Steam webhelper so repeated target packages no longer require manual ZIP installation.
- [Fixed] Reconcile overlapping QAM managed-setup monitors when another frontend instance has already consumed a successful completion, clearing stale progress/Cancel UI and refreshing the durable registered Cheat Engine identity without a duplicate notification.
- [Fixed] Prevent exact-session Auto-load from starting a duplicate Cheat Engine process, and revalidate the selected game/table/process plus detached-modal state immediately before any delayed automatic launch.
- [Fixed] Reconcile the detached Advanced view after Cheat Engine de-registration, and keep a failed managed reinstall visible while the prior verified installation remains usable.
- [Changed] Add a size/SHA-pinned project-local Chrome for Testing harness with exact-stage reruns, process-group cleanup, and JavaScript/DevTools readiness proof; browser absence now reports `INCOMPLETE` instead of a false QA failure.
- [Changed] Register the generic x64 Cheat Engine binary on new managed promotion instead of the short-lived CPU-dispatch launcher, while validating older installations against their recorded exact executable until an explicit reinstall migrates them.
- [Fixed] Keep private-runtime launch paths below Wine's legacy process-creation limit with one versioned aggregate identity key, and reject oversized executable/session paths before Proton starts.
- [Fixed] Load the resident bridge deterministically from the private runtime's generated `main.lua`, publish its first exact-identity heartbeat immediately, and resolve the active Gamescope display from owned target state instead of relying on Decky's inherited environment.
- [Security] Retire escaped Wine clients after every owned self-test through the exact selected Proton prefix and confirm that no client survives before reporting clean shutdown.
- [Fixed] Finish the controller workflow after a completed managed setup even if the optional read-only launch-capability refresh is slow; **Choose game…** no longer remains disabled after CE is already ready.
- [Fixed] Use Decky's native controller-aware confirmation dialog for **Reinstall CE**; equal-width custom button geometry alone did not change Game Mode directional navigation.
- [Fixed] Clear the local managed-setup progress snapshot as soon as the backend has registered and consumed a completed operation, preventing a stale **Extracting** spinner after a successful Reinstall notification.
- [Fixed] Turn managed-CE download failures into bounded actionable status: unavailable/refused/rate-limited/service HTTP responses, DNS/TLS transport failure, unsafe redirects, oversized data, unexpected installer format, and reviewed size/SHA mismatch all keep the prior installation intact.
- [Fixed] Make **Reinstall CE** download and re-verify a fresh reviewed artifact before extraction, while preserving the prior verified cache until the replacement download is safe to promote; the progress wording now states that refresh explicitly.
- [Changed] Keep paired custom modal actions visually equal-width; their directional controller behavior remains target-verified rather than inferred from CSS geometry.
- [Fixed] Remove an obsolete Proton-installer journal-cleanup tail that could raise an unhandled backend task error after a successful native CE registration; managed-extraction tests now require the completed worker itself to be exception-free.
- [Changed] Replace managed Cheat Engine setup through Proton with native, transactional extraction of the exact reviewed CE 7.7 installer; the installer is never executed and Proton remains launch-only.
- [Security] Validate the CE-specific loader, metadata CRC/XOR layout, payload SHA-256 values, confined output paths and final PE files before atomic managed-tree promotion.
- [New] Keep `tools/ce77_extract.py` as a standalone dependency-free CE 7.7 extraction and artifact-investigation tool; production uses an adapted backend module rather than a subprocess.
- [Limitation] Native extraction is coded and locally exercised on the exact CE 7.7 artifact, but C01 must still prove the packaged production helper under Decky permissions before release.

## 0.5.0 — 2026-08-23

- [Fixed] Confirm isolated Wine-prefix cleanup with the exact selected wineserver's wait result, tolerating its expected nonzero kill-race exit after every owned client is gone.
- [Fixed] Include Inno Setup's `/SP-` in the reviewed managed Cheat Engine invocation, preventing a hidden startup prompt from blocking controller-only Game Mode setup.
- [Fixed] Read Linux procfs process records with their real bounded descriptor semantics instead of rejecting their synthetic zero file size, and keep the setup picker open with an exact error when the backend refuses a start before creating an operation.
- [Security] Retire Wine clients that escape the installer launcher PGID through the exact selected Proton wineserver and journal-bound plugin-owned setup prefix, while tolerating `/proc` exit races without weakening marker checks.
- [Changed] Make the setup Proton choice explicit and setup-only, recommending an installed official Proton Experimental before custom tools and keeping that recommendation visible in a full-width selector, while runtime launch remains bound to the running game's observed Proton and prefix.
- [Fixed] Continue a surviving managed setup automatically after frontend reload, remove the redundant **Resume CE setup** action, and keep its progress indicator compact.
- [Fixed] Open **Choose game…** in a dedicated complete-library picker instead of dropping the normal workflow into Advanced diagnostics.
- [Fixed] Complete the Decky Loader 3.2.6 stripped-stdlib startup fallback with CPython 3.11.7 `html.parser` and `_markupbase`, after the exact XML-fixed target package exposed the next missing import.
- [Fixed] Start the backend under Decky Loader 3.2.6 by packaging the pure-Python CPython 3.11.7 XML stdlib and activating it only when Decky's PyInstaller runtime omits `xml.etree`.
- [New] Restore the controller-reachable advanced controls the compact rewrite had dropped: pin/unpin inside **Choose cheats…**, legacy startup-action visibility plus **Clear startup actions for this table**, **Forget the registered Cheat Engine**, and a plugin removal-readiness readout in Advanced.
- [Changed] Require one explicit confirmation before a managed **Reinstall CE** transaction starts, and tag the Cheat Engine card with **Managed**/**Imported** plus its compact SHA.
- [Changed] Keep session/descriptor wording out of the normal home panel; the exact stale-session reason stays under Advanced.
- [Fixed] Stop re-enumerating the whole Steam library on every running-game poll when the running AppIDs are already resolved.
- [Fixed] Bind Home's **Active Cheats** summary and **Choose cheats…** to the profile's exact current table SHA, so a partially failed activation can no longer label live results, or open the picker, with a cached inspection of a different table.
- [Fixed] Stop Home from handing the controller click event to its zero-argument workflow actions, so **Advanced…** now opens with the real library list instead of an event object as its game options.
- [Fixed] Reset the per-game table library together with startup, pins, consent, history and autoload when a profile's Steam/non-Steam identity changes, instead of leaving the discarded identity's exact table SHA associated.
- [Changed] Realign the repository fallback-bundle check and the architecture overview with the shipped 0.5.0 surface: Steam launch-option planning is reachable only through the temporary target harness, and per-game association/autoload/remembered callables are now the asserted production surface.
- [Changed] Replace the monolithic QAM validation console with a compact current-game home: Cheat Engine status/setup, running game, active table, confirmed active cheats, exact-SHA autoload, and one Advanced entry point.
- [New] Detect one unambiguous running Steam or non-Steam library game automatically, with an explicit library picker fallback when the Steam running-session API is unavailable or ambiguous.
- [New] Move online table search into a controller-focused modal with an editable game query, normalized provider results, local-artifact markers, and exact-table review before authorization.
- [New] Add a paged/grouped cheat-selection modal whose Apply action queries current state, sends only required runtime mutations, waits for exact ACKs, re-queries, and persists only confirmed state.
- [New] Add per-game table-library associations and opt-in **Load last table & cheats** state; remembered controls are stored per exact table SHA and never remapped to a different table version.
- [Changed] Make managed Cheat Engine reinstall an explicit controller action while preserving the previous validated installation until the replacement is atomically promoted and revalidated.
- [Fixed] Harden the compact workflow against stale UI/runtime state: manual game and Proton choices are not overwritten by background state, local-table review failures stay contained, same-table reconnect restarts the owned CE process instead of attempting a duplicate launch, and Advanced diagnostics refresh inside the detached modal.
- [Fixed] Keep **Active Cheats** based on a complete current-session query rather than the bounded status-result window; Apply mutates only controls the user actually changed and persists bounded exact-SHA remembered state only after verified readback.
- [Fixed] Final workflow bug-hunt now drops stale automatic game selection as soon as running-game context becomes ambiguous, restores exact-PID attach in Advanced, serializes detached-modal actions, keeps managed-install cancellation reachable after Proton selection, and binds multi-batch query/mutation/verification to one exact runtime session before any write.
- [Fixed] Make failed managed-CE replacement non-destructive even when rejected-tree cleanup fails, and treat cleanup failure of the hidden previous tree after a validated commit as recoverable rather than falsely reporting reinstall failure.
- [Fixed] Preserve fully populated schema-3 table history during schema-4 migration and later table-library eviction, and resume an already running managed install/reinstall after plugin reload instead of starting a competing installer.
- [Fixed] Cross-component UI stability pass now serializes Search→Review handoff, invalidates stale Home cheat snapshots during modal refresh/apply, binds Home/Advanced readiness to one exact prepared/status runtime identity, reconciles detached Advanced library/game/Proton/runtime state without close/reopen focus churn, and refreshes Home after partial table-activation failures.
- [Fixed] Final UI/backend cross-check fixes empty cheat pagination (`0/0` instead of a phantom page) and keeps the read-only target state helper aligned with production stale-session/owned-process semantics without mutating recovery state.
- [Fixed] Block controller Back/Close while detached Review/Cheats/import/setup/Advanced commits are in flight, and latch Search selection during its association→Review handoff so a dismissed modal cannot complete or reopen a hidden state change.
- [Fixed] Preserve static parent-before-child table-path ordering for live Apply mutations instead of sorting MemoryRecords by numeric ID; per-record value writes still precede activation and remain exact-session ACK/re-query verified.
- [Changed] Refresh the Steam Machine execution contract around the 0.5.0 Home/Advanced flow, add bounded `target_state_probe.py` correlations, remove obsolete Prepare/Refresh/Launch/Retire instructions, and make campaign audit require named C01/C03/T8 state captures before those gates can PASS.
- [Fixed] Make `qa.py` classify `frontend-core` as `INCOMPLETE` when the pinned TypeScript toolchain is absent instead of reporting a misleading project failure.
- [Fixed] Consume a successful managed-CE operation only after its exact executable identity is durably registered, so frontend reload no longer leaves a permanent **Resume CE setup** action after completion.
- [Security] Reserve the CE identity/materialization boundary for the complete asynchronous managed setup lifecycle, including the pre-operation start window; a surviving setup blocks competing CE import/runtime/session/autoload actions until it is resumed, completed, failed, or cancelled.
- [Security] Keep table import separate from execution consent: **Use this table** is the explicit exact-SHA authorization boundary, and autoload is allowed only for the already authorized exact SHA and confirmed target process.
- [Limitation] The new modal focus order, SteamOS OSK return focus, running-game observation, managed reinstall, attached launch, and live cheat apply/autoload paths remain Steam Machine target-gated by T0/T1/C01/C03/T8.

## 0.4.2 — 2026-08-22

- [Security] Keep managed Cheat Engine installer ownership in a durable, marker-bound process-group journal; a reload retires only the exact recorded group before reusing its Proton prefix, and an incomplete `/proc` scan now blocks cleanup.
- [Fixed] Treat a delayed resident-bridge acknowledgement as an indeterminate pending outcome, retaining the affected control reservation until reconciliation rather than inviting a duplicate mutation.
- [Fixed] Make startup control batches transactional by restoring earlier mutations when a later action fails; retain bounded table-preference history by evicting the oldest non-current entry instead of rejecting later table choices.
- [Fixed] Bind provider downloads to their individual search snapshot, move expensive acquisition inspection off the controller event loop, and always close provider network resources during cleanup.
- [Security] Bind each attached Cheat Engine lifetime to a freshly prepared session, reject stale pre-launch authorization, require exact PID-plus-basename attachment when a process name is ambiguous, and disregard heartbeats that predate the launched process.
- [Security] Serialize launch-supervisor snapshots and lifecycle transitions across Decky worker and event-loop threads, preserving a consistent owned-process/recovery view for controller polling.
- [Fixed] Stop owned Cheat Engine when the original game process set exits; recovery and stop now prove the complete POSIX process group is gone before clearing durable ownership.
- [Fixed] Preserve literal hyphen values in the bridge protocol, stop startup actions at their first failure, expose corrupt table-catalog metadata, and retain controller launch status only for the selected game.
- [Security] Made owned launch and recovery fail closed: an attached launch now requires the Proton identity observed from the running game, independently resolves the Steam client root, durably records ownership or aborts, accepts only an exact CE/table/descriptor heartbeat, and never treats an unreadable process or failed stop as a confirmed exit.
- [Fixed] Let a cold self-test use the backend's full startup window and report success only after its complete process group is gone; restart-free table switching no longer rejects the safe fresh heartbeat left by the exact owned process it just stopped, and launch readiness refreshes after CE setup/import changes.
- [Fixed] Corrected the C03 probe and runbook to use Proton's `run` verb for the no-game self-test, require an explicit enumerated Proton tool ID, and record effective UID, prefix ownership and surviving-process evidence.
- [Fixed] Repaired the resident Cheat Engine bridge, which could never have parsed a descriptor or a command: its canonical-integer check used regular-expression alternation, which Lua patterns do not support, so every AppID, generation and MemoryRecord ID was rejected. Control-character guards no longer rely on the removed `%z` class either.
- [New] Run the exact shipped bridge under a stock Lua interpreter against a Cheat Engine API stub, driven by the production descriptor/control renderers and validated by the production status parser, so protocol behaviour is provable off target; a static guard now rejects regex-only syntax in the bridge.
- [Changed] Read the registered Cheat Engine identity from the plugin's own configuration in the launch probe instead of requiring a hand-typed path, and allow a cold plugin-owned Proton prefix more time to come up before a self-test is called failed.
- [Changed] Made the Steam Machine runbook self-checking: every command it prints is verified against the real scripts, it now states the repository readiness phase, forbids modifying the target operating system, and requires reinstalling a freshly packaged plugin before any retest of a fix.
- [New] CE Decky now starts Cheat Engine itself through an exact installed Proton tool, using Valve's own `run` verb for the plugin-owned self-test prefix and `runinprefix` for a running game's prefix, so the normal workflow no longer needs a Steam launch-option write or a game restart.
- [New] Added a controller and agent runnable Cheat Engine launch self-test: it starts the private Cheat Engine runtime in an isolated plugin-owned prefix with no game, requires the resident bridge heartbeat, and stops the process it started.
- [New] Cheat Engine can be launched into an already running game and stopped again from the QAM, which makes switching to another exact-SHA table a controller action instead of a reason to leave the game.
- [Changed] The Proton identity for an attached launch is observed from the running game's own environment instead of selected by guesswork; a selection that disagrees with the running game is refused.
- [Security] An attached launch requires the compatibility prefix independently resolved from Steam library metadata to equal the one the running game reports, sanitizes inherited Proton/Wine/Steam variables, keeps Cheat Engine in one owned process group, and retires that group on stop, cancel, plugin unload and game exit.
- [New] A Cheat Engine started for a game survives a Decky reload or plugin update instead of being killed mid-game, and stays controllable afterwards: the owned launch is recorded durably and reclaimed only after the exact descriptor identity is re-proved in the live process environment.
- [Security] Session replacement may skip the stale-heartbeat wait only when CE Decky owns the exact session's Cheat Engine process and has proven it exited; a heartbeat newer than that confirmed exit still fails closed.
- [Changed] Promoted C03 into the core target campaign, added `scripts/target_ce_launch_probe.py` so first Cheat Engine launch evidence can be captured without Decky, Steam mutation, a game or a human, and reduced T5/T6 to the optional sidecar route.
- [Fixed] Routed every remaining POSIX-semantics test file through the Linux backend gate, so a native-Windows development run no longer reports failures the runner itself expects to be unrunnable there.
- [Fixed] Routed `tests/test_session_retirement_safety.py` through the Linux backend gate; it asserts POSIX Wine `Z:` session behavior and could never pass on native Windows.

## 0.4.1 — 2026-08-22

- [Fixed] Restored the release-equivalent CI gate: the target-validation plan test still asserted the pre-0.4.1 gate order after C01 was promoted into the core campaign.
- [Fixed] Stopped reporting a successful unattended Cheat Engine installation as a timeout when Proton leaves a wineserver holding the installer's log pipe open.
- [Fixed] Kept the backend loadable when the managed release manifest or the installed Proton tools cannot be read, so the controller-accessible manual import fallback stays reachable instead of the whole plugin failing to load.
- [Fixed] Treated controller cancellation of automatic Cheat Engine setup as a benign no-op rather than a setup error.
- [Fixed] Stopped reporting a live resident bridge as disconnected during its own non-atomic status replace, which could abort an in-flight runtime batch.
- [Fixed] Surfaced a cancelled or failed background download/install through its recorded operation state instead of cancelling the caller's RPC.
- [Security] Validated the managed installer's owned staging directory on the cached-artifact path, not only after a fresh download.
- [Changed] Documented controller-driven table switching as the supported stable-v1 workflow, distinct from the excluded in-process swap, and made the authoritative GitHub Actions backend gate part of the agent workflow.
- [Fixed] Resume completed first-run setup after a frontend reload without rerunning the installer, and recover a corrupt plugin-owned installer cache without Desktop Mode cleanup.
- [New] Add controller-only first-run setup that downloads the reviewed Windows Cheat Engine 7.7 artifact, verifies its exact SHA-256, installs it unattended through an explicitly selected installed Proton tool, and registers the validated managed tree without Desktop Mode.
- [Security] Keep managed CE installation manifest-pinned, bounded, cancellable, isolated in plugin-owned staging/prefix paths, and fail closed before execution on any URL, host, size, hash, path, or Proton identity drift.
- [Changed] Promote C01 into the core target campaign and retain Import existing Cheat Engine only as a controller-accessible fallback.

## 0.4.0 — 2026-08-22

- [Fixed] Added a bounded Codex CLI Steam Machine capability preflight, made the target runbook require explicit Git-publication authorization, and made cleanup of the verified read-only sidecar fixture portable.
- [Changed] Exercise controller-page reachability through every successfully imported public direct-provider table in the opt-in live acceptance profile.
- [Fixed] Synchronized the checked-in Decky frontend bundle and made release packaging import the backend from a freshly unpacked isolated ZIP before accepting the artifact.
- [Fixed] Treat Decky file-picker cancellation in local CE/table import and provider browser handoff as a benign no-op instead of surfacing normal Back/Cancel as an error.
- [Security] Serialize temporary target-validation actions before React can repaint disabled state, and disarm reviewed launch plans when the Steam/shortcut identity changes even if the numeric AppID is reused.
- [Changed] Make Online Sandbox publish packets cap per-file and aggregate inline text, routing oversized payloads through isolated SHA-verified blobs while retaining the three-write fast path for small text checkpoints.
- [Changed] Split execution-environment guidance into standard local/Codex and self-contained Online Sandbox workflows; the sandbox path now detects its transport boundary explicitly and prefers one inline Git-tree publication over per-file blob calls.
- [Changed] Bound unpublished sandbox work with a publish-debt budget: once a coherent staged checkpoint passes focused validation, publication starts before further audit/research, and long sessions periodically checkpoint meaningful commits to the same PR.
- [Security] Snapshot local/archive and browser-selected inputs through stable regular-file descriptors before inspection/extraction, reject final source symlinks, and use one fail-closed POSIX directory-fsync boundary for persistent table/runtime/shortcut/session state.
- [Security] Bound each live runtime RPC batch to 64 commands, require the previous batch to be exactly acknowledged before another can be written, and require an explicit validated `.exe` target for retry attach.
- [Fixed] Use Decky `Navigation.NavigateToExternalWeb` instead of the private Steam browser surface, and make Steam AppDetails unregister cleanup unable to strand an otherwise completed read.
- [Security] Make provider downloads create their staging destination exclusively without following or deleting a pre-existing path; partial files owned by the transport are still cleaned up on failure.
- [Changed] Re-audit current Decky API/UI/lifecycle integration against the 2026 official template, current Loader, current `@decky/ui` 4.12.0, and actively maintained plugins before the first Steam Machine campaign.

## 0.3.0 — 2026-08-21

- [Changed] Make sandbox publication retry-continuous: a remote-head/blob verification failure aborts only that atomic attempt, preserves staged work, and requires a fresh manifest/retry until the PR head and authoritative CI are synchronized or a concrete external blocker remains.
- [Fixed] Normalize bounded control-page steps after pin/filter list shrink, close transient value editors when their navigation context changes, and keep live Active/Value mutations disabled until the exact record has a positive current-session result.
- [Changed] Reworked the QAM workflow around current Decky-native dropdowns, toggles, bounded pagination, and explicit editors so games, tables, archive members, processes, provider results, and supported table controls remain reachable without text-filter-only navigation.
- [New] Added ACK-aware runtime control writes plus a verified `Deactivate all supported active cheats` action that queries exact supported MemoryRecord IDs, deactivates only records reported active in bounded batches, and re-queries them before reporting success.
- [Fixed] Removed fixed first-12/16/24 UI reachability limits, excluded ambiguous/group-only records from mutation controls, added controller-friendly two-step profile removal, and wrapped the production panel in the current Decky UI error boundary.
- [New] Added a static real-table UI probe and regression coverage for large paged tables, nested groups, scripts, dropdowns, unsupported/duplicate IDs, controller selectors, runtime ACK handling, and provider archive/result navigation.
- [Changed] Revalidated frontend patterns against `@decky/ui` 4.12.0 and actively maintained Decky plugins from August 2026; no external plugin code was copied.
- [Security] Require an attached target for record query/Active/Value commands at both host RPC and CE-bridge execution boundaries, reject group headers as mutation targets, and fail closed on fresh or clock-skewed session replacement/retirement and profile deletion with a current prepared session.
- [Fixed] Preserve failed exact-ACK runtime state without reporting success, bind ACKs through descriptor/session/record identity, restore arbitrary values for editable dropdowns without string-sentinel collisions, and clear no-op startup preferences instead of emitting invalid null/null state.
- [Fixed] Invalidate provider busy/acquisition work across game changes, prevent async imports from crossing game or failed-status-refresh boundaries, bound the static table probe before hashing, and make Vitest discover both `.test.ts` and `.test.tsx` frontend regressions.
- [Changed] Added controller-native All/Pinned/group section navigation for large nested tables, validated runtime retry-attach by `.exe` basename instead of implying PID identity, and surfaced explicit startup parent-dependency diagnostics without auto-enabling records.
- [Fixed] Keep saved profiles recoverable when their selected table can no longer be inspected, remove dead read-only dropdown editors, reject invalid custom process paths before save, and cancel late provider acquisitions before they can cross a game-identity transition.
- [Limitation] Real Steam Input focus traversal, SteamOS on-screen keyboard behavior, live Cheat Engine Active/Value/dropdown round trips, and Lua-created runtime-only MemoryRecords remain target-gated under T0/T1/T8.

## 0.2.0 — 2026-08-17

- [Security] Persist the exact Steam launch ownership/recovery snapshot atomically in the backend before any possible CE Decky launch-option write; an unconfirmed persistence result prevents Steam mutation.
- [Security] Verify exact backend persist/clear receipts, fail closed on POSIX directory-fsync setup failure, reject same-AppID reacquisition while recovery is outstanding, and reject a second AppID recovery acquisition while another durable owner remains.
- [Security] Make recovery closure compare-and-clear against the exact restored snapshot so a stale frontend cannot delete a newer journal for the same AppID; prevalidate bulk recovery before mutation and stop at the first runtime restore/clear failure.
- [Changed] Treat `launch_ownership` as the single durable write-ahead recovery record across frontend/backend reloads and every pre-write or post-write crash point, avoiding a second recovery database or fallible PREPARED/APPLIED phase marker.
- [Fixed] Removed the persistence-plus-rollback double-failure window that could previously leave mutated Steam state recoverable only from mounted frontend memory.
- [Changed] Keep durable recovery after Steam preflight/write/readback failure and require the existing guarded restore path to reconcile and clear it; if Steam was never changed, restore safely resolves as unchanged.
- [Limitation] Off-target tests prove persistence ordering and fail-closed orchestration; live Steam mutation/readback and Proton/CE behavior still require the numbered Steam Machine target gates.

## 0.1.4 — 2026-08-16

- [Changed] Validated the complete off-target user workflow as one coherent path, from local/provider table acquisition through exact-SHA profile selection, consent, startup controls, session preparation, runtime status, rollback, and removal readiness.
- [Fixed] Restored saved exact-table inspection when a configured game is selected, blocked unavailable/unsaved table selections from runtime preparation, and fail closed when backend status refresh is lost instead of retaining stale readiness.
- [Fixed] Reset prepared/runtime UI state when Cheat Engine, table, target process, or profile execution identity changes, and added explicit exact-SHA execution-consent revocation.
- [Fixed] Cancel provider acquisitions when search/game context changes, another result supersedes them, or QAM unmounts; serialize acquisition polling and recover from backend-lost/expired acquisitions without leaving search permanently disabled.
- [New] Added exact-session-ID guarded retirement of prepared sessions while preserving immutable session evidence. Retirement fails closed while any fresh, non-skewed resident-bridge heartbeat exists, including when profile/table identity has already become stale.
- [Security] Execution consent can always be revoked even if the selected table blob later disappears or becomes corrupt; granting consent still requires a fresh exact-SHA verified blob.
- [New] Added backend end-to-end workflow regressions plus component-level QAM workflow coverage for saved-profile hydration, exact table switching, consent revocation, disconnected-session retirement, superseded acquisitions, and fail-closed status loss.
- [Limitation] Steam mutation, Proton sidecar execution, and resident Cheat Engine bridge lifecycle still require the numbered Steam Machine target gates; off-target retirement is deliberately conservative and does not claim live target behavior.

## 0.1.3 — 2026-08-16

- [New] Added a local TypeScript-only frontend fallback for constrained development environments while keeping pinned Decky Rollup authoritative and blocking stale bundles from packaging with a source digest.
- [Changed] Require fresh upstream source verification for material Decky, plugin, Steam/Proton, and Cheat Engine integration decisions when current source is available.
- [Fixed] Restored portable Linux CI coverage for simulated Windows browser discovery and hardened plugin unload against lifecycle races.
- [Changed] Made QA reruns stage-scoped and bounded, added deleted-file/root-commit change detection, and routed explicit POSIX pytest selections through WSL on Windows.
- [Changed] Made dependency bootstrap validate the pinned environment rather than trusting stale cache markers, reducing unnecessary restores and repeated validation work.
- [Fixed] Synchronized backend/runtime version identity and the default provider User-Agent with development metadata, and reject malformed network limits/retry values before I/O.
- [Changed] Updated GitHub Actions to current supported action runtimes while retaining the project's pinned Node and pnpm toolchain.

## 0.1.2 — 2026-08-16

- [Fixed] Prevented automatic Windows validation from running the known-incompatible POSIX full suite when a config change expands test scope.
- [New] Exposed the Playground provider countdown in QAM and persisted bounded cooldown inputs when its direct API returns HTTP 429.
- [Limitation] OpenCT still challenges direct anonymous topic and attachment requests; the direct-provider gate reports this explicitly instead of treating browser handoff as success.
- [Security] Playground countdown tokens and temporary CDN URLs remain backend-only, are never persisted or logged, and are revalidated by the shared HTTPS/DNS/redirect policy.
- [New] Added direct anonymous Playground acquisition with advertised SHA-256 verification, plus a bounded one-command acceptance profile that rejects browser or file-picker fallback.
- [Changed] Prioritized direct catalog-to-table acquisition over browser and file-picker handoff, with bounded one-command live diagnostics for provider development.

## 0.1.1 — 2026-08-16

- [New] Added a concise one-command QA runner with captured logs, exact failure reruns, lazy environment checks, WSL-aware Linux gating, and installed-Chrome headless discovery.
